- Sun 4 Oct 01:08 — Item (2) BUG-378: CAUSE FOUND by code reading (not reproduced): the open-time pipe upgrade runs at the first quiet moment after the drawing is created and took its pipe list from the ONE project record CE holds in memory (ProjectDataService.Current), which is not switched at open: it is still the previous drawing's (or empty). CE switches it later in the Inspector's activation step (its one-off quiet-moment handler is added at activation, after the upgrade's start-up handler), so the first open read the wrong list, found nothing, said nothing and never retried; reopening the SAME copy worked because its own record was still in memory. CDC7UPGRADE had the same flaw. FIX: draft #602 (head a90efcb9; failing tests 09c33e8a, did not compile), base rel-20261015d f45bbe15: one helper PipesOfThisDrawing (read-only TryReadProjectWithoutChanging, no switch/sync/save) used by both. Assumption: a drawing whose pipes exist only in an old outside record file still finds nothing (as before). TESTED pipe-object/upgrade net10 170/170, net48 0 errors; full suite NOT run (AutoCAD open). Cleaned b03k (git clean -fdX) and removed it; no worktrees of mine left. NOT tested in AutoCAD; driver check written in the PR (first open of a fresh DRV17_B1 copy must print the upgrade line). Opus review required. usage: not readable headless.

- Sun 4 Oct 10:08 — Item (5) RELEASE NOTE FOR TIM (plain English), Civil 3.0.27 to 3.0.30 and Manager 1.1.27 to 1.1.30, trains C-12 to C-16, all installed 4 Oct (C-14 went out inside C-15). Installed, NOT Tim-accepted; only item 1's vanishing rows were checked by Codex in real AutoCAD.
  1. **Pit Schedule:** rows no longer vanish after "Revert row override" in the second drawing you open (Codex: 30 rows stay 30). IL columns show the plan's level when CE has none stored. Every open starts with an empty search box.
  2. **Two drawings open:** CE no longer mixes one drawing's pits and pipes into the other (Pit Schedule, CDSYSTEM, slab edits, rainfall). A change waiting to save in drawing A is no longer lost when you work in drawing B.
  3. **Opening and closing:** closing AutoCAD no longer writes CE's record into a drawing. The first open of an older job upgrades its old pipes.
  4. **Edit 2 Pipes and locked layers:** Edit 2 Pipes puts your object snaps back as they were. A locked pipe or label layer is named in the message, not "records do not match".
  5. **Pipe roughness:** "CONCRETE PIPE" now reads as concrete (0.013), not plastic. Every roughness value now comes from Settings, same numbers as before.
  6. **Older metre-based jobs:** CDCALC's pre-fill and Design Review read pipe lengths correctly (they were 1000 times out).
  7. **Your settings file:** placing a pit, drawing a pipe or closing the Pit Schedule no longer rewrites it. Remembered sizes and values live in their own file.
  8. **Messages:** command-line lines print plain arrows and dashes. Council rules name the document and page. CDDIAG, the Settings and the Civil/Structural hovers say what they really do. The raingarden Inspector says when a saved area is out of date. Overlay labels are 250 mm tall in any drawing unit.
  9. **Paste:** pasting CE pits into a drawing with no drainage tells you to run CDREPAIR. A pasted pit keeps its cover text.
  10. **Manager:** plain English on every page. Every greyed button says why when you hover. Typing CEMANAGER brings back a minimised window.

- Sun 4 Oct 11:57 — RELEASE NOTE FOR TIM (plain English), trains C-12 to C-17. C-12 to C-16 are installed (Civil 3.0.30, Manager 1.1.30). C-17 is put together but NOT installed yet. None is Tim-accepted.
  1. **Pit Schedule:** rows no longer vanish after the row-override undo in a second drawing. That button now reads "Stop overriding" (C-17). IL columns show the plan level when CE has none stored. The search box starts empty.
  2. **Typing in the Pit Schedule (C-17):** a pit number you type, even "C2", stays exactly as typed. Typing a level that pulls a held-grade pipe off its grade now tells you so.
  3. **Two drawings open:** CE no longer mixes one drawing's pits and pipes into the other. A change waiting to save in one drawing is not lost when you work in the other.
  4. **Pipes (C-17):** a flat pipe moved or gripped onto other pits keeps the levels you typed. Automatic relink keeps an open-ended pipe running downhill. The long section shows grade the same way as everywhere else, and blank when it is steeper than 1 in 1.
  5. **Older metre-based jobs:** CDCALC, Design Review and four more checks (C-17) read pipe lengths correctly. Before, they were 1000 times too short.
  6. **Settings and roughness:** placing pits, drawing pipes or closing the Pit Schedule no longer rewrites your settings file. "CONCRETE PIPE" reads as concrete (0.013).
  7. **Opening, closing and locked layers:** closing AutoCAD no longer writes into a drawing. Edit 2 Pipes puts your object snaps back. A locked layer is named in the message.
  8. **Messages:** plain arrows and dashes, including the CDCHECK list (C-17). A refused source pick says "pick again" once. Council rules name the document and page.
  9. **Paste:** pasting CE pits into a drawing with no drainage tells you to run CDREPAIR. A pasted pit keeps its cover text.
  10. **Manager:** plain English on every page. Greyed buttons say why. CEMANAGER brings back a minimised window. The module page's next step and CEHEALTH name the real buttons (C-17).
